%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%%%%%  Final call for computing score for left.  %%%%%

stepLength_tree1(1.0).
stepLength_tree2(1.0).
stepLength_tree3(1.0).
stepLength_tree4(1.0).
stepLength_tree5(1.0).

logPrior(-1.8).
left(D, Total) :- // A general accessor. 
   left(D, 1000000, Total), !.
left(D, Total) :- waitHere("This should not fail", left(D, Total)).

left(D, TreesToUse, Total) :- // A tree-limited accessor (e.g., for tuning the number of trees to use).
   logPrior(LogPrior),
   getScore_left_tree1(D, TreesToUse, Total1),
   getScore_left_tree2(D, TreesToUse, Total2),
   getScore_left_tree3(D, TreesToUse, Total3),
   getScore_left_tree4(D, TreesToUse, Total4),
   getScore_left_tree5(D, TreesToUse, Total5),
   Total is LogPrior + Total1 + Total2 + Total3 + Total4 + Total5,
   !.
left(D, TreesToUse, Total) :- waitHere("This should not fail", left(D, TreesToUse, Total)).

getScore_left_tree1(D, TreesToUse, 0.0) :- 1 > TreesToUse, !.
getScore_left_tree1(D, TreesToUse, Total1) :- left_tree1(D, Total), stepLength_tree1(StepLen), Total1 is Total * StepLen.

getScore_left_tree2(D, TreesToUse, 0.0) :- 2 > TreesToUse, !.
getScore_left_tree2(D, TreesToUse, Total2) :- left_tree2(D, Total), stepLength_tree2(StepLen), Total2 is Total * StepLen.

getScore_left_tree3(D, TreesToUse, 0.0) :- 3 > TreesToUse, !.
getScore_left_tree3(D, TreesToUse, Total3) :- left_tree3(D, Total), stepLength_tree3(StepLen), Total3 is Total * StepLen.

getScore_left_tree4(D, TreesToUse, 0.0) :- 4 > TreesToUse, !.
getScore_left_tree4(D, TreesToUse, Total4) :- left_tree4(D, Total), stepLength_tree4(StepLen), Total4 is Total * StepLen.

getScore_left_tree5(D, TreesToUse, 0.0) :- 5 > TreesToUse, !.
getScore_left_tree5(D, TreesToUse, Total5) :- left_tree5(D, Total), stepLength_tree5(StepLen), Total5 is Total * StepLen.
